**D-285 (2026-11-14): Address lookup on the zoning guide.** Jared: "we are also missing that feature where you can type in the address and then it will give you exactly what zoning it is". /zonings.html#zd-lookup promised it but only had a contact form. Now: the visitor types an address, the Worker (/api/zoning, src/worker/zoning.js) asks the BC Address Geocoder which municipality and parcel it is, then asks that municipality's own public map for the zone, ALR status and lot size at that point. Covered: Surrey, White Rock, City of Langley, Township of Langley, Abbotsford (their open map services, each checked live on Nov 14 against known addresses: 15850 26 Ave Surrey CD, 1500 Foster St White Rock CD-6, 5503 206 St Langley C1, 1265 235 St Langley RU-3 at 4.9 acres, 30736 Burgess Ave Abbotsford A1 at 19.5 acres). Nothing is guessed: if the province only matches to the block (common on acreages) the municipality's address data is asked for the exact lot, and if that fails no zone is shown and the page offers to have Jared check it. The result links to that zone's card in the guide when there is one. No key, no cost, the address is not stored. The contact form stays, folded under "Rather have me check it?". Delta, Chilliwack, Maple Ridge and Mission are in the guide but not in the lookup yet.

**D-286 (2026-11-14): Script allowlist, step 1 (report-only).** Jared approved tightening the security settings. The Worker now also sends a full Content-Security-Policy in report-only mode: own files, plus Google Maps, Google Analytics / Tag Manager, Cloudflare Turnstile, the MyRealPage listing frames, YouTube and Google Fonts. Report-only blocks nothing, so it cannot break the map, forms or search; the browser console lists anything the policy would have blocked. Step 2, after the live console is clean on the map, a listing search, a form, a video and the reviews page: move the same text to the enforcing header (another PR, Jared's yes).

**D-287 (2026-11-14): Script allowlist, first live reading.** With the report-only policy live, ten page types were loaded and every outside address they call was listed. Missing from the first draft: Cloudflare's own visitor analytics (static.cloudflareinsights.com), the Google Ads tag (googleads.g.doubleclick.net, doubleclick.net, google.com) and analytics.google.com without a subdomain. Added. Still report-only: the map, Turnstile, video and listing frames load on scroll or click and are checked by hand before enforcing.

**D-288 (2026-11-14): Map search page.** Two things. (1) On phones the Quick search box was hidden in the HTML and switched on by script, which pushed the page down as it loaded (layout shift 0.15 in PageSpeed); it is now in the page from the start. (2) The page's title promises zoning and ALR, so the address lookup from D-285 now also sits under the map ("Check a property's zoning and ALR status"); the lookup box is one shared component (ZoningFind.astro) used there and on the zoning guide.

**D-289 (2026-11-14): Zoning lookup used in three more places (Jared: "go ahead").** (1) Listing pages: 144 of Jared's 203 listings now show a "Zoning and land" block: the zone (with a link to what it allows when the guide has that zone), ALR status, and lot size for a house or land on its own lot when the listing gives none. The data is src/data/listing-zoning.json, read on Nov 14 through the live /api/zoning for every listing address (tools/listing-addresses.json); 153 matched, 48 are in municipalities the lookup does not cover, 2 did not match, and 9 that already state their zoning keep their own. It is a dated snapshot ("checked November 14, 2026"), refreshed by re-running the pass. (2) Lead emails: when a form carries an address (home evaluation, contact), the email gets one "Zoning:" line with the zone, ALR status and lot size; no match or a slow map adds nothing and never delays the lead (5-second cap). (3) Farm, acreage and land pages: the address box itself is in the zoning block, instead of a link to the guide.

**D-290 (2026-11-15): More from the address lookup, and a real lead form behind it.** (1) A result now also shows the Official Community Plan land use (Surrey, City of Langley, Abbotsford, White Rock) and whether the lot is inside the city's mapped flood area (same four); the Township of Langley publishes neither as a public layer, so those rows are left out there. Layers checked live on Nov 14 (15850 26 Ave Surrey "Low-Rise II"; 4688 King George Blvd "Agricultural", in the flood-prone area; 5503 206 St Langley "Historic Downtown Core"; 1500 Foster St White Rock "Town Centre"; 30736 Burgess Ave Abbotsford "Agriculture 1 - Uplands"). The lead email's Zoning line carries the OCP and a flood flag too. (2) "Get a full zoning report" replaces the email link on a result: it opens a short form with the address filled in. The old "send me the address" form asked for an address and an optional name only, so there was no way to reply; it now asks for an email (required) and phone (optional), and the email subject is "Zoning report request". The form sits under the lookup on the zoning guide, the map page and the farm and acreage pages.

**D-291 (2026-11-15): Address lookup covers Maple Ridge and Delta.** Both cities publish their zoning as open map services (Maple Ridge DataCatalog/PlanningDevelopment, Delta DeltaMap/Planning), checked live against known addresses (21555 Dewdney Trunk Rd RM-1; 11995 Haney Pl C-3; 11713 94 Ave Delta RD3; 6469 Meadow Pl Delta RS5; 5008 47A Ave Delta CD435-1). Maple Ridge also gives the OCP land use; Delta links each zone to the city's own PDF. No flood layer for either. Chilliwack and Mission are not added: the only Chilliwack zoning service found is a third party's copy, not the City's, and Mission's was not found.

**D-292 (2026-11-15): "Jared has sold here", from his own deal records.** Jared sent his seller-deal folders for 2017 to 2026 (about 370, "all sales mostly") and said: no counts per area ("would that look bad if it's low in areas?"), but yes to saying he has sold in an area, a building or on a street. So: (1) the Miramar Village, Morgan Crossing, Belaire and Forest Ridge building pages say he has listed and sold homes there, with a matching FAQ; (2) the three sell pages (Langley; Surrey; South Surrey and White Rock) get "Where Jared has sold": the street names and complex names, no house numbers, no counts; (3) a lead email with an address gets a "Past sales:" line when it is in a building or on a street he has sold. The site holds only src/data/sold-streets.js (street and complex names by area). The deal list itself, with house and unit numbers, is not in the repository, which is public. Cancelled, lease and unclear rows were left out; a named street is filed under a city only where the records or the site's own sold listings agree (Meadow Place and Sandon Drive were mis-filed as Langley in the list and are corrected). Other cities are in the data for the lead email but are not shown on pages, where a short list would read as thin.

**D-293 (2026-11-15): Buyer-side deals and neighbourhoods.** Jared sent his buyer deal folders too (384, 2017 to 2026) and asked whether sub-areas would help. Wording rule: a seller deal is "has sold homes here"; a buyer deal is "has helped buyers purchase here"; never a count. (1) 36 neighbourhood pages get one line under the H1, for example "Jared has sold homes in Murrayville and helped buyers purchase here." The neighbourhood for each deal comes from the regional street grid (a numbered address such as 4657 208A Street gives its own position) matched to the site's own area map polygons, and only when the point is at least 250 m inside the boundary; deals on named streets, near a boundary, or outside Langley, Surrey, Cloverdale, South Surrey / White Rock, North Delta and Maple Ridge are not used (about two thirds of the rows). White Rock is taken from the city in the records. (2) Building pages: Miramar Village, Morgan Crossing and Forest Ridge now say both sides; Belaire stays seller side. (3) Lead emails: the "Past sales:" line knows buyer-side buildings as well. Left out everywhere: withdrawn, backup, lease, referral, assignment and unclear rows (several are client names), and the deal lists themselves, which are not in this public repository. Also fixed: the farm-page zoning block used a random id, so about 60 pages got a new sitemap date on every build; the id is now fixed and an unchanged build changes no dates.

**D-294 (2026-11-15): Second pass for the neighbourhood lines (Jared: "go ahead with the second pass").** The 158 deals the street grid could not place (named streets with a city in the records, and numbered addresses near a boundary) were looked up with the BC Address Geocoder in Jared's browser and matched to the same area polygons, counted only on an exact civic-number match at least 100 m inside a boundary: 92 placed, 43 outside every mapped area or too close to an edge, 23 not matched. Result: 55 neighbourhoods in all (19 added, among them Abbotsford East, Central Abbotsford, Poplar, Sumas Prairie, Fort Langley, Fraser Heights, Sunnyside Park, the Chilliwack and Sardis areas and Mission; four went from one side to both). Rows with no city in the records were not looked up: a street name alone can match the wrong town. Fort Langley's page is a hand-built one and does not show the line yet.

**D-295 (2026-11-15): Sold homes on the neighbourhood pages.** Jared asked whether the "sold here" lines help SEO: a little on their own (first-hand experience, a unique true sentence), more when backed by content. So the neighbourhood pages that carry the line now also show "Homes Jared has sold in X": up to three of his sold listings in that MLS® sub-area, newest first, street or complex name only and no price, linking to the sold listing page. These are the same cards the guides already had (D-211); 39 more pages get them (114 in all), where the site has a sold listing in that sub-area. Not done: reviews by place. Only three of the Google reviews name a place (Langley, South Surrey, White Rock) and the site already picks reviews per page type, so there was nothing worth building.

**D-296 (2026-11-15): "Sold" claims now rest on Paragon closed sales only.** Pete's Paragon export of Jared's listings for 2017 to 2026 arrived: 378 listings, of which 198 closed, 109 were terminated, 69 expired and 2 are pending. The deal folders used in D-292 to D-294 held all of these, so some "has sold" lines rested on listings that never sold. Corrected from the export: (1) a neighbourhood page says "sold" only when Paragon has a closed sale in that MLS® sub-area. Four lost the line (Chilliwack Proper South, Cultus Lake, Fraser Heights, Serpentine), four keep only the buyer wording (Elgin Chantrell, Hazelmere, Poplar, Sumas Prairie), and 19 with a closed sale gained it (Yaletown, Nordel, Dunbar, Sapperton and others); 71 in all. One closed sale reads "has sold a home", two or more "has sold homes"; the count itself is never shown. (2) The streets on the sell pages are now the streets of closed sales only (Langley 40, South Surrey 30, Surrey 28, White Rock 9) and the lead-in says "closed MLS® sales". (3) 49 complexes where Jared listed but did not close a sale no longer appear as "sold" (nine of them stay as buyer-side only). The four building pages (Miramar Village, Morgan Crossing, Belaire, Forest Ridge) each have a closed sale and are unchanged. Buyer-side lines still come from the deal folders: Paragon's export is listing side only.

**D-297 (2026-11-15): Jared's closed sales listed on the neighbourhood pages.** From the Paragon export (198 closed sales, 2017 to Nov 2026), each neighbourhood page and guide with a "Homes Jared has sold in X" section now also lists the rest of his closed sales in that MLS® sub-area under the photo cards: month and year, home type, street name, beds, baths, floor area, and "sold in N days" when it sold within three weeks. A sale that has its own listing page on the site links to it (115 do); the other 83 are plain lines. No house or unit numbers and no prices (Jared asked what is best; the recommendation he accepted: prices raise privacy and board-rule questions and add little for search, days on market sells better). The first eight show, earlier ones fold under "Show earlier sales". 62 pages carry a list. Not built: individual pages for the 83 sales the site has no page for (no photos or description, so thin pages). Data: src/data/sold-paragon.json; refresh by re-running from a new export.

**D-298 (2026-11-15): Own listing prices follow MLS.** Jared: "shouldn't the listing be attached to what happens on MLS?" The search pages read MyRealPage live, but the pages under My Listings are files in the repository (a snapshot from Oct 31), so the Rosemary Heights price drop had to be typed in by hand. Now a GitHub Action (.github/workflows/listing-sync.yml, tools/sync_listings.mjs) runs twice a day: it reads Jared's active listings from his public MyRealPage page and, where an Active listing on the site shows a different price, writes the new price to the listing page and the homepage card, builds and tests the site, and pushes to main, which deploys it. This is the one change that goes live without Jared saying "merge" each time; merging this pull request is his yes to that. Guards: prices only; "Original Price" is never touched; a jump of more than half either way is reported, not written; an empty or unreadable MyRealPage page changes nothing; a failed build or test pushes nothing. A listing that left the active list, or a new listing with no page, is reported in a GitHub issue ("Listings need a look"), because sold dates, photos and descriptions need a person. Checked by hand on Nov 15: all 10 active listings on MyRealPage match the site; 116 Calgary Avenue (Penticton) is Active on the site but not in the MyRealPage active list.

**D-299 (2026-11-15): Sold listings update themselves.** Second step of D-298 (Jared: "is it not able to automatic update like the old website?"). When an Active listing on the site is no longer in Jared's MyRealPage active list, the twice-daily job looks for it in his MyRealPage sold list; if it is there, it reads the sold date from the listing's own MyRealPage page and marks the site's page sold (status, the Status line, a Sold Date line) and takes its card off the homepage. The site is built and tested before anything is pushed. A listing that left without selling (expired, cancelled, pending) is still only reported, as is a new listing with no page. Written and tested against real MyRealPage pages fetched on Nov 15 (tests/fixtures). 116 Calgary Avenue, Penticton is on the Okanagan board, not in the feed, and is on the kept-by-hand list (Jared, Nov 15: for sale, re-listed at $1,279,000).

**D-300 (2026-11-15): Fixes from the Nov 15 full review.** Jared reviewed the findings and approved the "real" ones. (1) Sitemap dates: the Cloudflare build has the Google Maps key and a local build does not, so ~850 pages hashed differently live and got the build day as `<lastmod>`. The manifest is now written with `npm run lastmod` (a stand-in key of the same shape), `data-key` is normalized and non-ld+json scripts are left out of the hash. (2) Leased listings no longer print the masked rent "$[***]" in their description, and the commercial FAQ says the rate is not published. (3) Live review dates on /reviews.html use the same "20 April 2026" form as the written list (Vancouver time). (4) Two short Google reviews that were missing from the written list were added (89). (5) The listings box waits 45 s before showing its fallback and comes back if listings arrive late. (6) Two dead outbound links fixed or removed (Surrey Schools, chilliwack.com). (7) Map page wording for the link to Jared's own listings. (8) Report-only CSP allows *.myrealpage.com for scripts, styles, fonts and connections. Not done (not approved): image sizes, web manifest, http links.

**D-301 (2026-11-15): Review and polish of the Nov 12 to 15 work.** Jared asked for a review of the last few days. The built site was scanned (1,932 pages: stray text, duplicate titles and descriptions, heading counts, internal links, generated "sold" lines) and the new sections were looked at on phone and desktop; nothing wrong in the content. Polish: (1) "More of Jared's sales here" list lines up as date + line, with the date on its own row on phones; (2) the zoning guide's call/email line stacks cleanly on phones; (3) ten stray screenshot files removed from the repository root; (4) listing sync stops without changing anything if more than half of the active listings are missing from the MyRealPage page (a bad read), its issue is only added to when the report changes and is closed by the job when nothing needs a look, and the workflow and commit are named for what they do now (prices and sales).

**D-302 (2026-11-15): New listings make their own pages; small fixes Jared approved with "go".** (1) The twice-daily listing sync now builds a page for a listing that is active in Jared's MyRealPage list and has no page here: heading, price line, facts, description, room tables, video, photo captions, a homepage card, the postal code and the old-address redirect id, all read from the listing's own MyRealPage page (`buildListing`, `writeListing` in tools/sync_listings.mjs). It only writes when the page has a title, city, price, description, Active status and at least one photo; otherwise the listing stays in the report as NEW with the reason. Photos for these pages load from MyRealPage's photo server (`u` entries in src/data/listing-photos, read through src/lib/photo-url.ts) until they are processed into the site's own storage like the rest; this is the one exception to "never hotlinked from the vendor". Every page made is listed in the sync issue so it gets a look. (2) Reviews page intro states the rating and count and what is on the page. (3) Zoning guide description names the address lookup and White Rock and Maple Ridge. (4) Web manifest and 192/512 icons added. (5) Homepage portrait gets a 720-wide copy. The listing photo sizes were left alone: the pages score 97 to 100 and changing them would re-date all 200 listing pages. (6) 36 outbound links moved from http to https after each host was checked on https; 17 were left (host not confirmed or dead).

**D-303 (2026-11-15): Filler wording removed from 155 page files.** Jared: "Is there any fluff to remove? Any words not needed?" then "Go ahead". Old-site copy in src/data/pages only: (1) "…and contact Jared today for …" sign-offs and standalone "Contact Jared Gibbons …" / "Work with local Realtor Jared Gibbons for expert guidance …" lines removed (every page has call, text and home-value buttons); (2) "Whether you're … X delivers" sentences removed, and the "Whether you're searching in A, B or C, explore every X real estate opportunity in one convenient place" lines rewritten as "Search every X real estate listing in A, B and C" (keywords kept); (3) the templated Abbotsford hub sentences shortened; (4) "stunning", "vibrant", "world-class", "unparalleled", "nestled", "hidden gem", "showcase" taken out, with about 20 sentences rewritten by hand. No facts were added. Left alone: client reviews, MLS listing remarks, blog posts, the neighbourhood guide files (src/data/local, where such words are quotes from a city or developer), and the one-line note under the review strip (changing it would re-date about 1,500 pages for a few words).

**D-304 (2026-11-16): Freshness fixes (Jared: "Fix everything").** From the first weekly freshness check (docs/review/freshness/2026-11-16.md). (1) Past "expected" dates on 11 pages replaced with what the primary source says now, or dated where the source itself is stale (Burnaby Lake fields, Mackin Park, Mission Tea House, BC Hydro Ranch Park, FVRD workshop, Township water/sewer review, Jackson St care home, Fleetwood Athletic Park, The Warwick FAQ, election count). (2) 2026 tax rates: Abbotsford Bylaw 3727-2026 incl. Sumas Prairie dyking levy, Maple Ridge 2026 rates, Belcarra Bylaw 641-2026; Township of Langley stays on 2025 until tol.ca publishes 2026. All in tools/fix_freshness_2026_11_16.py with sources. (3) Condo building pages: the four shared "before you buy" bullets became one building-specific line plus a link to /bc-condo-buying-rules.html, so the 20 pages the uniqueness gate would have held (53–69%) are now 72–89% and stay indexed; the "More condo buildings nearby" list now takes the next 8 in the same city (wrapping round), so every building page has links from its siblings, not just the hub. Fixes ride on branch freshness-2026-11-16 because main is live.

**D-305 (2026-11-15): Page-two keywords, first batch: mobile and manufactured homes.** Jared: "Ok do 1" (push the keywords ranking 11 to 20). Search Console, 28 days to Nov 13 (79K impressions, 1.19K clicks, average position 11.2): the seven city mobile-home pages are the largest group sitting just off or low on page one, about 5,800 impressions and 90 clicks (Langley 1,998 at 7.6; Abbotsford 1,308 at 17.3; Surrey 817 at 11; Mission 679 at 8; Maple Ridge 470 at 8.7). The searches they miss are the general ones: "manufactured homes for sale", "new mobile homes for sale", "modular homes for sale", "mobile home dealer". Each of the seven pages gets a section "New, used, manufactured or modular homes for sale in {city}" and three questions (new homes, manufactured vs mobile vs modular, is Jared a dealer), sourced to the Province, BCFSA and Technical Safety BC; no tax or licensing claims. The Langley page's H1 changes from "Affordable Mobile Homes for Sale in Langley, BC" to "Langley Mobile and Manufactured Homes for Sale" to match its title. Next groups from the same report: zoning guide (974 at 7.1), Clayton guide (519 at 8.6), Abbotsford farms (498 at 19.6), South Surrey open houses (421 at 9.5), Langley acreages (387 at 10), Surrey and Langley court-ordered sales (362 at 18.4, 343 at 11.6), and the "white rock realtor" searches (about 350, positions 6.5 to 11.5, answered by the homepage because no page targets them). (Numbered D-304 in its commit and pull request; renumbered here because the freshness fixes took D-304 the same night.)

**D-306 (2026-11-15): White Rock realtor page.** Jared: "Build it". Search Console (28 days to Nov 13) shows about 350 impressions for "white rock realtor", "white rock real estate agent", "realtor white rock" and "real estate agent white rock" at positions 6.5 to 11.5, answered by the homepage because no page targeted them. New page /white-rock-realtor.html (tools/build_wr_realtor_page.py -> src/data/local/white-rock-realtor.json): recognition lines as on the South Surrey agent page, the White Rock streets and buildings Jared has sold in as listing agent (names only, no counts, seller side only), the live White Rock listings box (same search as /white-rock.html), the White Rock price table already on the guide, selling and buying sections that link the White Rock pages, and six questions. Linked from the About menu, the White Rock listings page, the White Rock guide and the South Surrey agent page. (Numbered D-305 in its commit and pull request.)

**D-309 (2026-11-16): "Helped buyers purchase here" now rests on Paragon.** Pete's export of Jared's buyer-side sales (183 closed residential, 2017 to Nov 2026; plus 3 commercial purchases) replaced the deal folders as the source for every buyer-side claim, as D-295 did for the listing side. The workbook is a client record and stays out of this repository; only names changed in src/data/sold-streets.js. A building counts when a closed buyer-side sale is at its civic address (or the complex name matches); a neighbourhood counts when a closed buyer-side sale is in its MLS® sub-area. Result: 5 neighbourhood lines removed (Bear Creek Green Timbers, Elgin Chantrell, Hazelmere, Panorama Ridge, Poplar: no Paragon buyer-side sale), 3 cut back to "sold" only (Annieville, Crescent Beach & Ocean Park, Fleetwood Tynehead), 18 gained "and helped buyers purchase here", and 16 neighbourhood pages gained a line for the first time (Bradner, Holly, Vedder Crossing, Boundary Beach, Steelhead, Port Guichon, Eastern Hillsides and others). Buildings: 13 buyer-only claims removed (Serenade, The Loop, The Hudson and others), 8 cut back to "sold" (Forest Ridge, Kitchner, Holden Residences and others; the Forest Ridge page text too), 18 gained the buyer side (Belaire, Allure, Willsbrook at Southridge Club and others; the Belaire page text too). Progressive Plaza stays, from the commercial buyer-side sale. Pending sales do not count. Commercial sold prices were not requested: the site shows no prices.
**D-308 (2026-11-16): One name per kind of section.** Jared approved: "Related pages", "More real estate nearby" and "Nearby areas & related pages" (the nearby-area link lists, about 500 pages) are all "Nearby areas to look at"; the archive card grid "Explore More" (about 100 pages, plus the Fort Langley and reviews pages) is "Also worth a look"; "Sources for the home-type notes" is "Sources". Done at render time in GenericPage.astro, so the data files and builders are untouched and a re-run of any builder keeps the new names. A second list or sources section on the same page becomes "More nearby areas" / "More sources", so no new heading repeats on a page. The "Ready to get started?" call to action stays (Jared: keep it). The sitewide rotation of the research-section headings was tried and parked (branch plain-headings, not for merge): small, uncertain gain for re-dating about 520 pages.
**D-307 (2026-11-16): Fixes from Cursor's SEO copy review (PR #26, report only).** Jared: "Yes go". (1) Breadcrumbs: tools/build_breadcrumbs.py now names the last step after the heading the page actually shows (seo-final.json `h1`, when 60 characters or less) instead of the archive H1, which on about 90 pages was an old sentence ("Every active Abbotsford home listing, newest first"); 154 trails change, all to their page heading. Cloverdale and Mission hubs no longer read "Home > Cloverdale > Cloverdale" with the middle step on the houses page: they sit under Surrey and Communities. 75 neighbourhood pages whose trail ended "X real estate" while the heading says "Homes for Sale" now end "X Homes for Sale" (edited in src/data/local; the tool that wrote them is not identified). After the change every page's visible breadcrumb matches its schema, and no trail repeats its last step. (2) "Ask for the The Beverley" (and Heights, Semiah, Summit) fixed in the four building pages and in tools/build_building_pages.py. (3) The sold Greenside Estates, Cloverdale post had a South Surrey open-house description; replaced. Not done: the presale city pages were reported thin, but each lists its own 21 to 25 projects with developer, deposit and completion year and its own sold data (2,000 to 2,400 words; only the two-sentence intro is shared), so they stay separate and are not folded. The Aldergrove article's address is left as is (changing it needs a redirect for little gain).

**D-313 (2026-11-16): Pets, rentals and age rules by complex.** Jared: "yes go ahead. make sure its easy to understand and we wont have to always change them". Source: Pete's workbook of condo and townhouse complexes in Langley and South Surrey / White Rock (built from MLS® listing fields, checked November 2026). The workbook stays out of this repository (/home/claude/private-sales/in); tools/build_strata_rules.py turns it into src/data/strata-rules.json. Only facts that rarely change are kept: name, type, year built, number of homes (only when a listing states it), and pets / rentals / age limit as one of "Yes", "Yes, with limits", "No", "55+", "No age limit", "Ask me" (listings in the same complex disagree) or "Not stated". Fees, prices, pet counts and rental caps are left out because they change and the listings often disagree. Every table says it comes from listings, not bylaws. GenericPage.astro adds the table at the end of 24 condo and townhouse pages (Langley neighbourhoods, South Surrey neighbourhoods, White Rock, Crescent Beach & Ocean Park, the four 55+ pages; tables over 15 rows start folded) and three rows to the building-facts table on 17 condo building pages. Complexes listed under two names at one address, or spelled two ways, are merged; differently named buildings sharing an address stay separate. Rerun the tool when Pete sends a new workbook; nothing else needs editing.
**D-312 (2026-11-16): Buyer-side deals are "Purchased", not "Sold".** Jared: "for the 55 buyer side, can we call them purchases?" 55 of the old site's "Sold" listing pages were deals where Jared represented the buyer (Paragon buyer side, Nov 16 export). They carry `"side": "buyer"` in src/data/listings and now show "Purchased: Jared Gibbons represented the buyer." under the price, titles such as "4885 64 Street, Ladner BC – House Purchased | Jared Gibbons", "Purchased" on cards and in lists, and no "Listed by ROYAL LEPAGE NORTHSTAR (W.R.)" line (that brokerage was not the listing brokerage). They no longer appear under "Homes Jared has sold in X" or among the sold homes on the selling page; the sold page says "Jared's most recent sales and the homes he helped buyers purchase". Found in the same pass and fixed: the sold page's older-sales list showed a count per city ("Ladner (1)") and full house numbers; the sold page, My Listings and the selling page cards showed full house numbers for sold homes, and the selling page cards showed a price under "Sold". All now show the street or complex name only, no count and no price, as the rules for sold homes require.
**D-311 (2026-11-16): New-construction and presale pages open with their own facts.** From Cursor's duplicate-page review (PR #26, report only); Jared: "do 1 and 2 and presale cities like you recommend". The 85 new-construction pages opened with the shared listing-alert form (city pages) or a sentence describing the search; each now opens with its own "newest homes that sold" line, moved up from lower on the same page (Cloverdale: duplexes, built 2024, 9 sales, median $1,173,000; Ladner: Neilsen Grove condos). The 7 presale pages open with their own count: "I'm tracking 25 presale projects in Surrey scheduled to finish between 2027 and 2029: 16 in 2027, 6 in 2028 and 3 in 2029" (from the "at a glance" paragraph, which stays). Vancouver opens with its own project count, not the Renfrew resale duplexes Cursor suggested. Also fixed: on 74 of these pages the sentence placed before the listings box said "the search above"; it now says "below". Done at render time in GenericPage.astro, so the builders and data files are unchanged. Not done from the same review: retitling 98 neighbourhood pairs (Search Console shows no competition, e.g. Willingdon Heights has no impressions in 90 days) and removing White Rock from the homepage title (the homepage has 1,574 White Rock impressions at position 8.4 against 220 at 39.4 for the White Rock page; revisit once /white-rock-realtor.html is indexed).
**D-310 (2026-11-16): "Jared has sold in X", without "a home".** Found in the full check of Nov 16: 32 neighbourhood pages said "Jared has sold a home in X", which tells the reader it was one sale (the count rule, D-296, is meant to avoid exactly that). All lines now read "Jared has sold in X", "Jared has sold in X and helped buyers purchase here" or "Jared has helped buyers purchase in X" (Jared's own wording, Nov 15). Place names written "Holly in Ladner" are now "Holly, Ladner" (12 names), which also reads better in "Homes Jared has sold in …".

**D-314 (2026-11-16): Pet-friendly condo and townhome pages; rentals column dropped.** Jared: "yes go ahead" to pet-friendly, rental-friendly and 55+ pages from Pete's complex workbook. Built: four pages, /south-surrey-white-rock-pet-friendly-condos-for-sale.html, /south-surrey-white-rock-pet-friendly-townhomes-for-sale.html, /langley-pet-friendly-condos-for-sale.html and /langley-pet-friendly-townhomes-for-sale.html (tools/build_pet_pages.py). The fixed words are in the page files; the counts, the "no pets" and "cats, not dogs" lists and a table per neighbourhood (pets, dogs, cats, how many) are added by GenericPage.astro from src/data/strata-rules.json, so a new workbook updates them with no editing. BC rules quoted from the Province's strata pet bylaw page and BCFSA (Bill 44). Each page's listing box reuses the area's condo or townhome search (all listings, labelled as such). Linked from the four South Surrey and Langley condo and townhome hubs and from every page with a complex table. Not built: rental-friendly pages. Since November 24, 2022 a BC strata cannot ban or cap long-term rentals (Bill 44; BCFSA), so every complex is rental-friendly and the listings' rental field is out of date; the Rentals column is removed from the D-313 tables and building pages, replaced by one sentence saying so. 55+ pages: not needed, the four existing 55+ pages already carry the complex table (D-313). Pet facts on the building pages now show dogs and cats.

**D-315 (2026-11-16): Mobile home park type and 55+; MHPTA details.** From Pete's mobile home park rules workbook (kept private), as approved. tools/build_park_rules.py writes src/data/park-rules.json: for each park, how the spot is held (pad rental, co-op shares, strata lot, own the land; left blank when the workbook is unsure or the park mixes MH and RV) and 55+ only when every MLS® listing that fills the age field says 55+ (at least two) or the park's own documents say so. GenericPage.astro adds a Type column to the 11 area tables on /mobile-home-parks-fraser-valley.html and writes "55+ (per listings)" in Notes where the existing note is blank (58 of 71 parks matched by civic number and street); the note under the tables explains both and the Human Rights Code test. Not published: pad rents, buy-in fees, pet rules (mostly unconfirmed listing marketing), the municipal zoning figures the workbook marks "secondary summary, confirm", and the Surrey, Mission and Maple Ridge redevelopment policies (not re-read at the source this session). /bc-mobile-home-buying-rules.html gains three paragraphs read in the Manufactured Home Park Tenancy Regulation itself (tools/add_mhpta_details_2026_11_16.py): the section 48 grounds for refusing an assignment, keeping the sale an assignment so the rent carries over, and the section 31 (existing pets) and section 3 (no guest fee) rules.

**D-316 (2026-11-16): 34 neighbourhood house and condo-townhome pages.** The pages on Pete's list the site did not have (Delta 21: Tsawwassen and Ladner sub-areas; Chilliwack 10; Crescent Beach and Ocean Park houses, its condos and townhomes, and White Rock townhomes). Gerry saved one MyRealPage search per page (IDs 1452186 to 1452220, sheet kept in /home/claude/private-sales/in). tools/build_nb34_pages.py writes each page from figures already in the repo: the Paragon closed-sales aggregate for the sub-area (same 5-sale minimum), the busiest complexes for condos and townhomes, and a comparison with the city (median, days to sell, a table of its communities). Condos and townhomes share a page as everywhere else; White Rock condos already had one, so White Rock gets townhomes only. Each neighbourhood page and city type page links to the new pages ("Search by home type" / "Search by neighbourhood", src/data/nb-type-links.json, added at build time). The two South Surrey / White Rock strata pages also get the D-313 pets and age table. Sitemap dates: the quick-search option list (data-qs) is no longer part of the page hash, since adding one search page to it re-dated about 950 pages; the hashes were refreshed once with LASTMOD_REHASH=1 and no date changed.

**D-317 (2026-11-16): No offer markup on sold or leased listings.** Jared approved taking the listing schema off sold pages on October 1 (MyRealPage era); the new build had put it back on all 203 listing pages, marking sold ones SoldOut (report forwarded 2026-11-16). ListingPage.astro now adds the Offer (price and availability) only to active listings. The rest of the RealEstateListing markup stays on sold pages: address, photos, MLS® number and, on 100+ sold pages, the video tour, which is what lets Google show those videos. Leased pages already had no Offer.

**D-318 (2026-11-16): No prices on sold listing pages.** Jared: "yes take off the sold prices". All 190 sold listing pages (purchases included) showed a price at the top, in the facts ("Price", "Original Price", "MLS® & Price") and, on 135, in the search snippet ("Sold Jul 2017 for $1,420,000"); the summary price on a sold page is the sale price. ListingPage.astro now shows no price on a Sold page: not at the top, not in the facts, not in the snippet or structured data, and no payment calculator. Active listings keep their price and leased ones their rent. Left as is: the area statistics block (current listing averages for the area) and figures inside the listing remarks (assessed values, rents).

**D-319 (2026-11-16): Pets and age tables for Tsawwassen, Ladner and Chilliwack; Chilliwack pet-friendly pages.** Pete's second complex workbook (493 complexes from 588 active and 973 sold condo and townhome listings; kept in /home/claude/private-sales/in) feeds the same tool: tools/build_strata_rules.py now takes both workbooks and tags each complex with its region. The D-313 table is added to the 13 new Tsawwassen, Ladner and Chilliwack condo-townhome pages (D-316), the Tsawwassen, Ladner, Chilliwack and Sardis condo and townhome pages (long ones folded) and the two Chilliwack 55+ pages (55+ complexes only; 55+ is now the only age label, as BC voids lower strata age limits). Two new pages, /chilliwack-pet-friendly-condos-for-sale.html and /chilliwack-pet-friendly-townhomes-for-sale.html (19 Chilliwack condo buildings say no pets in their listings), built like D-314. Tsawwassen and Ladner get no pet-friendly page: almost every complex allows pets, so the page would say little. Also fixed: six Chilliwack and Sardis pages (and one search snippet) described "55+ and 45+" complexes; a strata age limit under 55 has been void since November 24, 2022, so they now say 55+.

**D-320 (2026-11-16): Pet-friendly pages show pets-allowed listings only.** Gerry saved six MyRealPage searches filtered to "pets allowed" (IDs 1452223 to 1452228: South Surrey / White Rock, Langley and Chilliwack, condos and townhomes), replacing the all-listings searches the six pet-friendly pages borrowed in D-314 and D-319. The line above each listing box now says the listings are the ones whose MLS® listing says pets are allowed. His counts (November 16): South Surrey / White Rock 498 of 551 condos and townhomes, Langley 613 of 640, Chilliwack 174 of 197.

**D-321 (2026-11-16): Less repeated wording on listing and school pages; a list of pages to redirect or hide.** From a sitewide check of shared wording (every sitemap page: the share of its words in sentences that appear, numbers aside, on 5 or more other pages). Area pages were already distinct (10 to 15%, mostly the shared contact and review blocks); the look-alikes were the 203 listing pages (median 64%), 203 blog posts (54 over half), 160 school pages and 85 new-construction pages. Jared approved: (1) Listing pages: the "Explore more searches and neighbourhoods" block (about 300 links' worth of words, more than the listing itself) is cut to two columns of five links on listing pages (ExploreLinks compact); sold and leased listings with a video show it right under the summary; an empty price slot no longer leaves a gap. Listing pages over half shared fell from 172 to 55; the MLS® board disclaimer stays (required). (2) School pages: the "How the search works" paragraph and the general checklist items (confirm the catchment, visit at drop-off) moved to one new page, /buying-a-home-near-a-school-bc.html (tools/build_school_explainer.py, with the district catchment finders); each school page keeps a one-line summary with a link, and its own checklist items (its median, its nearest other school). About 90 repeated words per page. (3) New-construction pages: no shared explainer to cut; their repetition is the lead forms (VIP access, listing alerts, quiz, call to action), left as is. (4) Blog posts and thin sold listings: Search Console, 12 months to November 14, 2026, gave the clicks for each; a review sheet (/home/claude/private-sales/out/pages-to-redirect-or-hide-2026-11-16.xlsx) recommends keeping 14 posts with clicks, redirecting 11 zero-click posts with a clear match (the listing itself, market stats, acreage, farm and condo/townhome pages) and hiding 29 from Google, and hiding 46 sold listings with no video, no clicks and 0 to 2 photos. Nothing is redirected or hidden until Jared marks the sheet.

Search Console (12 months): none of the 40 zero-click posts ranks in any real sense (13 never shown, 18 shown under 10 times, 9 shown 10 to 135 times), and the 46 thinnest sold listings were never shown. No redirects. (2) Blog posts (tools/build_post_updates.py -> src/data/post-updates.json, applied in the post route and the sitemap): 8 articles get a dated update and stay in Google: the SkyTrain guideway post (B.C. government, September 21, 2026: guideway about 65% built, opening 2029), the October 2025 Costco post (links the November 2025 approval post), the July 2025 market update, the townhomes-under-$800K and condos-under-$420K posts (12-month medians), the neighbourhood-by-neighbourhood post (links each neighbourhood page), the Langley acreage and the Langley "why now" posts. 18 old listing ads (new listing, price drop, sold) get "this listing has ended, see homes for sale now" and leave Google; 6 past-event posts leave Google. Supersedes the hide and redirect list in D-321.

**D-328 (2026-11-17) Copy clean-up after a full-text scan of the built site.** Jared: "do all three. and then check again after" (typos and spelling, unreadable source-card titles, research-note wording).
- Typos: "ontact Jared Gibbons!" (Ugly Potato Day post, now one link "contact Jared Gibbons!"), "a age" -> "an age" (2 Pitt Meadows 55+ pages), "in a and walkable" / "in a and emerging" (2 listing posts; the missing adjective is dropped, not guessed). - Source cards (src/lib/sources.ts `tidy`): upload hashes, UUIDs, media-library suffixes and upload timestamps are removed from file-name titles; "%20 written as 20" is decoded; dropped apostrophes (Surreys, Burnabys, Canadas, Authoritys) and slug acronyms (Mis, Ismp, Dpa, Servicebc) are fixed; a title that is only a code ("2014b2400140", "P3", "Tt503") becomes what it is ("City bylaw", "Council report", "Route 503 timetable", "Court decision 2025 BCSC 1553"). A meta.ai link on the Southwest Maple Ridge 55+ page was removed.
- Research-note wording: new tools/fix_note_wording.py (runs after scrub_research_wording.py; idempotent) removes or rewords note labels and asides that reached pages: "Question 1 for Jared", "The page cannot agree with itself", "How people search for it" (30 list items), "Buyers also search", the 55+ boilerplate "This page names each building…" (46 pages), "cross-reference only, never land", "the BC strata block" -> "BC strata rules", "recorded locator run" -> "School Locator check", "(Ministry-listed names only)", "NAME NOTE", "Spelling lock", "Tenure record only", "labelled correctly", "— POINTER", file names left in brackets and dangling "… in." where a page name had been cut, plus capitals used for emphasis (LEASEHOLD, FEE-SIMPLE, IF …). Changed 230+ local pages. Monthly order: run it after `scrub_research_wording` and before `npm run build`.
- Not done: the local sections still read in a research-note register in places (long "label: fact; fact" lines, bylaw section numbers). A plain-language rewrite of those sections is a separate, larger job.

**D-329 (2026-11-17) Plain-language rewrite of the research-note sections, South Surrey and Langley first.** Jared: "Yes go ahead and do that" (rewrite the local sections that still read like research notes, area by area, starting with the core pages). Scope: the 264 South Surrey / White Rock and Langley local pages; every visible sentence over 90 characters that scored as note-register (semicolon chains, section citations, "label: fact" openers, note words such as frame, carried, documented, recorded, shelf, ground) was rewritten by hand in plain buyer-facing English: 308 sentences (310 places, as some repeat). Facts, numbers, names, addresses and dates are kept; section-number citations are dropped where the Act or bylaw name says enough; nothing new is asserted. Two corrections came out of it: Willoughby's Bylaw 6201 boundary is now described as proposed (it has had first and second reading only), and a truncated school list (County Line Glen Valley) and a cut-off bylaw number (Langley City SSMUH, Bylaw No. 3284) are completed. The rewrites live in tools/data/plain-rewrites.json, keyed by the generated sentence, and tools/fix_note_wording.py applies them, so they survive the monthly regeneration; a sentence whose source note changes keeps its generated wording until it is rewritten. Next areas, in order: Surrey (Cloverdale, Clayton, Fleetwood, Newton), then Abbotsford, Maple Ridge / Pitt Meadows, Delta, the Tri-Cities and Vancouver / North Shore.

**D-342 (2026-11-17) Plain-language second pass, Maple Ridge and Pitt Meadows, Delta, and the Tri-Cities.** Same method as D-339 to D-341: about 275 lines for Maple Ridge and Pitt Meadows, 202 for Delta (Ladner, Tsawwassen, North Delta) and 311 for Coquitlam, Port Coquitlam, Port Moody, Anmore and Belcarra, all in tools/data/plain-rewrites.json. "Board sub-area" is "real estate board area", presale lines say "seven days to cancel" without the section numbers, the ALR housing limits use the one standard sentence, and "no X was confirmed" is "I don't know of any X". Removed: a stray "Email check. The correct email is ..." line that had leaked onto five pages (Riverwood, Sapperton, South Meadows, Shaughnessy, Thornhill), and "historic subdivision fabric = built lot fabric, not offerings" / "assemblies: none documented as inventory" lines on the Chineside, Harbour Chines, Eagle Ridge and Hockaday pages. Repaired: a Port Moody condo line cut off mid-sentence at "run between St." (now says only that the CPR tracks run through Moody Centre), and a Barber Street heritage line missing its "Confirm" ("...631 Ioco Road register status with the City"). HPI benchmark lines and "Draft for Jared's approval" notices are untouched.

**D-343 (2026-11-17) Plain-language second pass, the remaining regions: Burnaby, Vancouver, the North Shore, Richmond, New Westminster and the general pages.** Same method as D-339 to D-342, finishing the second pass: Burnaby (319 lines), Vancouver and the West Side guides (258), North and West Vancouver with Squamish-area guides (183), Richmond (135), New Westminster (142) and the general and BC-wide pages (42), all in tools/data/plain-rewrites.json. As before: "board sub-area" is "real estate board area", "stock"/"shelf"/"frame"/"diligence" are replaced with plain words, presale and age-bylaw lines drop their section numbers, "no X was confirmed" is "I don't know of any X", and inventory shorthand reads "N for sale and M sold". Vancouver and North Shore pages that explained at length why SnapStats has no figures now say only "I don't have market figures for X: the monthly market report I use doesn't cover it." Removed: "historic subdivision fabric = built lot fabric" lines, bare "(SnapStats, September 2026)" headings, "Both statements are carried; neither is upgraded" and "Basis: name-verifying current listings" notes, and White Rock bylaw numbers that had been pasted into West Vancouver pages as "never attributed here". Repaired: Mount Currie lines that began mid-sentence ("21) and trust deposits"), an Uptown zoning line with a stray "M.", a West End school line that began "40's (SD40)", an S.W. Marine line cut off at "quoted for S.W.", and a North Arm line that referred to "(3)" of a list that was not there. Left alone: HPI benchmark lines, the photo credits page and one line on the listings page about a listing shown under another board's MLS® number.

**D-345 (2026-11-17) Any MLS® listing opens inside the site.** Jared: "can we have it where there is a box of the listing inside the page? I want it to all work together." Until now only Jared's own listings had pages; any other listing link under jaredgibbons.ca went to the search page (D-231), and the only working link for someone else's listing was on idx.myrealpage.com. Now `/recip.html/listing.<slug>.<id>` (the same `listing.…` tail MyRealPage's share link carries) is answered with one template, src/pages/mls-listing.astro, which loads MyRealPage's detail view for that listing in the vendor's self-sizing frame, with the site header, a heading built from the address in the link, and a call and showing line under it. Tested in Chrome on the live site with 1496 240 Street, Langley before building. The page is noindex and not in the sitemap: these are other brokerages' listings that change daily. Jared's own listings are unchanged (the Worker still sends their ids to their real pages), and old listing links under an area page (`/murrayville.html/listing.…`) still redirect to that area page as in D-231. Known limits: the vendor's frame gives no signal when a listing has sold or been removed, so a dead link shows MyRealPage's own "not found" inside the box with the search link under it; and the "Login with / Signup" bar and the sign-in required for sold listings are inside MyRealPage's frame, where the site's CSS cannot reach (sold data behind a sign-in is a board rule).
**D-344 (2026-11-17) Brokerage name: Northstar to Little Oak on every listing page.** At Jared's request, the listing credit on 174 listing pages (173 sold, plus the active Penticton listing) now reads "Royal LePage Little Oak Realty" in place of the feed's older names "ROYAL LEPAGE NORTHSTAR (W.R.)" and "Royal LePage Northstar Realty (S. Surrey)"; co-listing brokerages on three sold listings are unchanged. tools/sync_listings.mjs applies the same replacement on import so a sync does not bring the old name back. Two answers on the listings page that described the Penticton listing as "listed by a different Royal LePage office" were rewritten. Not changed: "NorthStar" as the developer of Porthaven in Port Coquitlam and the Northstar Montessori school link, which are unrelated, and the research notes and test fixture under docs/ and tests/.

**D-346 (2026-11-17) MyRealPage's login bar hidden in the listing frames.** Jared: "take away all the logins everywhere". MyRealPage has no setting for the "Login with Facebook / Google / X · Signup · Login" bar (checked in the account, Nov 17), but its listing pages load public/idx-embed.css from this site (D-244), so the bar is hidden there for visitors who are not signed in: on listing detail pages (including the new /recip.html/listing.… page, D-345), in the detail view opened from the map, and on the search form. Left in place: the "Signup / Login" button on sold listings (the boards require an account to see sold data, and it is now the way to create one), a signed-in visitor's own toolbar, and the small account icon beside the map filters.

**D-347 (2026-11-17) MyRealPage listing pages in the site's black and white, and share links on jaredgibbons.ca.** Jared: "its blue and ugly" and "can you not change the link somehow in the back end?" (1) Style: public/idx-embed.css, which MyRealPage loads inside its own pages (D-244), now also covers the listing detail page: Montserrat and Lato, black tabs, icon row, share row, link list and buttons in place of the default blue, square corners, and black buttons on the map's sold cards and pop-up forms. Previewed on a live listing in Chrome before shipping. Rows that printed a "Signup" link where a held-back value would be are hidden (D-346). The map's blue cluster bubbles are drawn by MyRealPage's script, not CSS, and are unchanged. (2) Links: new file public/idx-embed.js, to be loaded by MyRealPage through Listings > Settings > Advanced > Custom Footer Content (one script tag; the old account's footer held the Facebook Pixel the same way). It rewrites the share buttons' addresses to https://jaredgibbons.ca/recip.html/listing.…, and when a listing on idx.myrealpage.com is opened on its own (an old share link, a link in a listing alert email) it sends the visitor to the same listing on jaredgibbons.ca (D-345). It does nothing until that script tag is added. (3) A pasted "#!listing.…" link on any page of the site now opens the listing's own page; clicking a listing on a search page still opens it in place.

**D-349 (2026-11-17) Listing pages: share row, homes for sale now, printable feature sheet, showing buttons that stay on screen.** Jared asked for the four things his listing pages lacked next to the best agent sites. (1) Share row under the action buttons: Copy link, Text, Email, Facebook and Print feature sheet. (2) "More homes for sale" (on a sold page, "Homes for sale now") above the closing call to action: up to three of Jared's active listings as photo cards, same city and home type first, then nearest in price, with buttons to the area's search page, all of Jared's listings and the full search. The existing list of other homes Jared has sold in the city stays. (3) Feature sheet: "Print feature sheet" prints the page through a print layout (or saves it as a PDF from the print dialog): address, price, main photo and four more, floor plan, description, facts in two columns, room table and Jared's contact line; menus, buttons, video, maps, the calculator, the vendor's area statistics and the long disclaimer are left off. About five pages for a house with full MLS® detail. It is the browser's print, not a designed PDF file per listing. (4) Phones: once the buttons under the price scroll away, Book a showing, Text and Call (on a sold page, Ask about homes like this and Call) stay at the bottom of the screen; on listing pages this replaces the site-wide bottom bar. Checked in a local build at desktop and phone width; listing photos do not load in the build sandbox, so the photo cards and the photo part of the print were not seen with real images.
**D-348 (2026-11-17) Search page shows listings, small source lists, MLS link removed.** Jared's review of Nov 17. (1) "Search Listings" (/recip.html) opened a framed map on desktop: "map sucks to use". It now shows the quick search (area and home type) and the South Surrey listings as photo cards at every width, as phones already did; the map is the "Open the map search" button (/map-search.html, unchanged). (2) "Sources? looks odd": a Sources heading with one or two sources no longer gets its own full-width folding band with a single card; the sources close the section above as one small line (123 pages). Three or more sources keep the cards. (3) The "Multiple Listing Service (Realtor.ca)" link is off the buyer and seller resources page: "that doesn't help anyone".

**D-350 (2026-11-17) Map search in the site's fonts.** Jared: "Can we change anything else? Font?" public/idx-embed.css now also covers MyRealPage's map search: Lato for the controls and listing cards, Montserrat for the name bar and prices, black for links, the orange search button and the email link. Previewed on the live map in Chrome. Not changeable from a stylesheet: the price pins and cluster circles (drawn on a canvas by MyRealPage), the map tiles, and the layout of the filter bar.

**D-351 (2026-11-18) Map search in the site's look.** Jared: "can you make it look like our style in the website?" A second pass on MyRealPage's map search in public/idx-embed.css: the name bar gets a black home button and a black rule under it; the search box and the Type, Price, Beds, Sold and More controls get black 1px borders with no shadows; the four round floating buttons become square and black; the listing cards lose their shadows and get bold addresses, grey brokerage lines and black NEW LISTING and SOLD ribbons in place of green and red. Previewed on the live map in Chrome. Unchanged, because MyRealPage draws them on a canvas: the teal and red price pins and the cluster circles.

**D-352 (2026-11-18) Five follow-ups Jared approved ("go ahead with all your suggestions").** (1) Search page introduction no longer promises "advanced filters" on the page; it says what the page does and that price, bedroom and MLS® number filters are on the map search. (2) Search result cards: "View photos" and "Schedule viewing / email" (D-262) are hidden, leaving one "Listing details" button per card; the MLS® number and description lines from D-262 stay. One CSS line in public/idx-embed.css reverses it. (3) "More homes for sale" on listing pages (D-349) now orders by the same home type, then the nearest price, then the same city; a sold page's own price orders its suggestions though it is not shown. (4) MyRealPage's listing detail page, second pass: the viewing form, mortgage box and area statistics take the site's form style (white, black 1px border, Montserrat capital labels, full-width black Send button), and the page's text is in Lato. Previewed on a live listing in Chrome. (5) Zoning address lookup: suggestions as you type, from the Province's BC Address Geocoder, limited to the seven municipalities the lookup covers, and picking one runs the check; the result now prints what the zone allows (the zoning guide's own summary lines, from the new /zoning-zones.json) with links to the bylaw section and the guide. Where the guide has no summary for a zone yet (R-1E in the Township is one), the result says so and links the bylaw section. 195 of the 278 zones carry only an intent or purpose line and 7 carry none, so fuller summaries remain to be written municipality by municipality.

**D-353 (2026-11-18) Summaries for the last seven zones with none.** Jared: "Can you write the summary for the missing 7 pages?" The zoning guide had seven zone cards with a name and a bylaw link but no text, so the address lookup (D-352) had nothing to print for them. Written from the bylaws on Nov 18, in src/data/zonings.json: Township of Langley R-1B, R-1D and R-1E (s.402), R-2 (s.410), MH-1 (s.411) and RU-13 (s.213), with minimum new lot sizes, frontage and depth from the s.110.1 table and the small-scale multi-unit housing conditions from the s.102 definition; City of Delta I2C (s.16.21). Each opens with the zone code and municipality, then homes allowed, other uses, minimum new lot, lot coverage and height or setback limits, in metric with imperial in brackets. Only what the bylaw states: no locations, no examples of where the zone applies. Delta's own bylaw file blocks automated reading, so I2C was read from the copy the City published on Let's Talk Delta (consolidated Oct 23, 2025) and the card says so. Every zone in the guide now has a summary; 195 still carry only the bylaw's one-line intent.

**D-354 (2026-11-18) Zoning guide: fuller summaries for every zone that can be read, White Rock and City of Langley added, lookup for Chilliwack and Mission.** Jared: "Can you please do this next and finish it?" (1) The 195 zones that carried only the bylaw's one-line intent now say what can be built: homes or main uses, other uses, minimum new lot, and density, lot coverage and height, in metric with imperial in brackets. Read Nov 18 from Surrey's consolidated by-law (59 zones, plus R1 and R2), Abbotsford's online bylaw (57 zones; SSMUH 1, SSMUH 2, SSMUH 3 and TH2 from the City's January 2026 SSMUH Application Guide because their bylaw pages block automated reading), Delta's October 23, 2025 consolidation (57 zones), Maple Ridge RS-2 and Mission RU80s and RR7s. (2) The Township of Langley's four group cards (RM, C, M and P zones) are now 54 cards, one per zone, so an address in any of them gets its own summary; P-2A to P-2R stays one card. The Township residential zones that had only a lot size (R-1A, R-1C, the seven R-CL zones, CRE-1, SR-2, SR-3A, FH-1) are filled in the same way from Section 400 and the s.110.1 table. (3) New guides: White Rock (Zoning Bylaw 2024, No. 2506: 21 general zones and the CD group) and City of Langley (Zoning Bylaw 2025, No. 3300, adopted March 9, 2026: all 20 zones). The guide goes from 278 to 370 zones and from seven to nine municipalities; the lookup links White Rock and City of Langley results to their cards. (4) Address lookup for Chilliwack (the city's map service; the current zone is in its CalcField, the ZONE field still holds 2001 codes) and Mission (the city's zoning layer, the Province's ALR polygons, no lot size because there is no public parcel layer). (5) A card that stands for several zones lists them in `covers`, and a numbered CD zone falls back to the municipality's CD card. Left with a one-line summary: the three CD cards (site-specific by nature), Abbotsford SSMUH 2-g, RHR, UCZ, NCZ and LIZ (pages block automated reading and no other City source covers them) and Delta MUL, MU6 and MU6R (added after the consolidation that could be read). Page title and H1 unchanged; meta description now names White Rock. Sources and raw notes: bylaw links on each card.

**D-355 (2026-11-18) Zoning: Vancouver, Burnaby and Coquitlam in the lookup; Pitt Meadows, Coquitlam and Vancouver guides; audit fixes.** Jared: "you should add vancouver, burnaby, coquitlam, tsawwassen, ladner, delta, mission, pitt meadows, maple ridge, sardis as well", and asked for a review of the Nov 17-18 combined site audit. (1) Lookup: Vancouver (the City's open data API, which is not a map server, so the Worker reshapes its answer), Burnaby and Coquitlam (their map servers), all three with the Province's ALR layer. Tsawwassen and Ladner addresses already resolve to Delta and Sardis to Chilliwack in the Province's geocoder, so they were covered; the page now names them. Pitt Meadows publishes no zoning map data (its map is a vendor app), so it has a guide but no lookup. (2) Guides: Pitt Meadows (Bylaw 2505, revised to Jan 11, 2022: all 37 zones and the CD group, with a note that it predates the 2024 small-scale housing rules), Coquitlam (Bylaw 3000: the eight Part 10 low-density zones in detail, legacy and apartment zones in brief, the rest grouped) and Vancouver (R1-1, R3, R4 and R5 in detail from their schedules; RT, RM, RR, commercial, industrial, historic and CD districts as group cards with `covers`). 370 to 441 zones, 9 to 12 municipalities. Not done: a Burnaby guide (the City's 2026 district guides need Jared to approve a web-access prompt, so Burnaby results show the zone code without a summary), and the urban residential zones of Maple Ridge, Mission and Chilliwack (their guides still cover farm and rural zones only). (3) Audit, fixed because true: in-article headings rendered at paragraph size (two competing .pg__h rules; now 1.25rem, h2 up to 1.75rem), guide panel headings (.pc__h) smaller than body text, breadcrumb links 16px tall on phones, the market ticker asking for an unloaded font weight, "rental voidness" (Central Park page) and "rezonable" (16216 20 Avenue post). Checked and left: the #1 claim wording ("by FVREB Medallion Club points" is what Jared supplied; the audit says "MLS points", so it is his call), the darker fine-print greys (more legible than the token), the quick-answer grey, the designed-page type scale, the review quoted as written, the 24 old posts missing from the sitemap (hidden on purpose, D-322), and four external links the audit saw failing (all four load today).

**D-356 (2026-11-18) Two blog posts from links Jared sent: the Peninsula Christmas events post expanded, and a new EV charging guide.** Jared: "go ahead, make sure they are seo friendly and add lots of value." (1) The Nov 13 post "Things to Do in White Rock & South Surrey" already covered holiday events, so it was expanded at the same address instead of adding a second post that would compete with it: new title "White Rock & South Surrey Christmas Events 2026: What's On", a dates-at-a-glance list, sections by type (lights, markets, Historic Stewart Farm, shows, kids), a free-events list and three common questions. Sixteen events were added from the Cloverdale Reporter's Nov 16 listing; the five Historic Stewart Farm events and the Indigenous Christmas Market were checked on surrey.ca, which corrected one date (Homemade Holidays is Dec 12 and 13, not Dec 14 and 15). The rest are credited to the Reporter with a "check before you go" line. (2) New post /blog.html/ev-charging-south-surrey-white-rock-guide: the BC Hydro hub at the Superstore (six 180 kW chargers, Oct 29, 2026 bulletin), the Tesla Supercharger in the same lot, the South Surrey Park and Ride hub, BC Hydro's rates since April 1, 2026, and what a condo or townhome buyer should check (owner requests under the Strata Property Act, majority-vote thresholds since Dec 6, 2023, the electrical planning report due Dec 31, 2026 in Metro Vancouver and the Fraser Valley, and BC Hydro's EV Ready rebates), each linked to its BC Hydro or Province source. The home-charger rebate amount is left out because the only figure found dates from December 2023. Both posts get black title cards in the Nov 13 style. The Langley "Live Here, Shop Here" piece was an advertising feature and was not used.

**D-357 (2026-11-18) Urban zones for Maple Ridge, Mission and Chilliwack; a Burnaby guide; an instant map snapshot on the home value form; twelve question pages; the script allowlist enforced.** Jared: "yes do these" (the More button, the urban zones, the home-worth form feeding Keyvera, short Q&A answer pages, the Burnaby guide, and the security setting). (1) Zoning: Maple Ridge Part 6 (RS-1 to RS-1d, SRS, RUR, RST, RST-SV, RT-2, RM-1 to RM-6, RE, RG, RG-2, RMH, with the s.402.29 Urban Infill unit caps), Mission chapters 6 to 10 (MM1, MD465, MB558, MMP, MR1, MT1, MA1, MA2, the UC, R and S zones and their "s" secondary-dwelling versions) and Chilliwack section 8 (R1-A to R9, RMH, RC, with the s.4.08(7) small-scale multi-unit rules), read from the consolidated bylaw PDFs; Burnaby Zoning Bylaw 2026 (Bylaw 14815, in effect July 1 2026), all 16 districts from the City's July 2026 summary guides and the R1 to R3 district PDFs, read in Jared's Chrome; sub-districts (R2r to R8r, I1a, P1a, P2a) point at their district's card. The address lookup now names the Burnaby guide (its map already uses the 2026 codes). 441 to 518 zones in 13 municipalities. (2) The map search "More" button that sits high is inside MyRealPage's own map frame (another origin), so the site's styles cannot reach it; left for MyRealPage, or it goes with a move to Repliers. (3) Home value form: the form already emails Jared and his Keyvera lead address (BL-05). New: after the visitor types the address and presses Next, step 2 shows what the municipality's public map says (zone and a link to the guide, lot size, ALR, floodplain, community plan) from /api/zoning, and the same lines go to Jared in the request's notes. Nothing shows where the lookup does not cover the area. The home evaluation page's title is now "What's My Home Worth? Home Evaluation, South Surrey & Langley". (4) Question pages (tools/build_qa_pages.py): property transfer tax, the newly built home exemption, the flipping tax, the speculation and vacancy tax, foreign buyers, the FHSA, the RRSP Home Buyers' Plan, the minimum down payment, the 2026 home owner grant, appealing a BC Assessment and the 3-day rescission period, each answering in its first paragraph with worked numbers, the official source (gov.bc.ca, canada.ca, bcfsa.ca, bcassessment.ca, read Nov 18 2026) and FAQ markup, plus a hub (/bc-real-estate-questions.html) linked from the footer. First-time buyer PTT and the GST rebate are not repeated (existing pages). (5) Security: the script allowlist from D-286 moves from report-only to the enforcing header after a clean check of the live home page, map search, a community search, the home evaluation form, a listing with video and map, reviews and the zoning lookup (no resource outside the list, no violation event).

The 511 Similkameen page is the recent Interior board sale, not the 2022 FVREB one, and shows no price.

**D-359 (2026-11-18) Site check: one duplicate page merged, competitor names out of the source lists.** Jared: "can you go through everything and find any issues". **D-362 (2026-11-19) Poultry farm pages: one job each, less shared text.** Jared: "how can we get those farm pages higher?" Search Console, Nov 11–17 against Oct 21–Nov 10: the poultry, broiler, chicken and Abbotsford poultry pages all slipped, and the query data shows why: the same searches ("chicken farms for sale", "poultry farm for sale", "chicken farm sale") now show four of our pages instead of one (the poultry page held "chicken farms for sale" at 1.3 before; now 15, with chicken-farms at 10.5 and broiler at 33). Part of the slip is the move to the new site (the whole site's average position moved the same week). Changes: (1) The broiler title goes back to the old one that ranked about 4 ("Broiler Farms for Sale BC | Turn-Key Grower Operations"); the new title had added "Chicken". The chicken-farms title drops "Broiler Farms with Quota" ("Chicken Farms for Sale BC | Quota, Barns & Land"). (2) Ten valley-wide pages carried the same 78-row zoning table, about a third of each page's text. Poultry, broiler, chicken, egg, turkey, the chicken-farm guide and the acreage rules page now show the address check and the search only (ZoningLookup `compact`); the table stays on farms.html, land.html, the Fraser Valley acreage page and the guide. Shared text between the poultry pages fell from 50–68% to 33–42% (what is left is the site-wide header, reviews and forms). The facts match the published quota post (Aug 21, 2026). The strata depreciation-report source is dropped from its list.
