**D-364 (2026-11-19) Mobile home pages: titles and snippets that earn the click.** Jared: "do the mobile home fixes". Search Console, Oct 21–Nov 18: the mobile home pages rank 4–14 with very few clicks. "mobile homes for sale langley" showed /langley-mobile-homes.html at position 4.4 for 296 impressions and 1 click. On Google the page sits second, between REW (which shows a price range) and Zillow (which shows listing prices), with a generic snippet. Changes: seven pages (Langley, Abbotsford, Surrey, Maple Ridge, Mission, Chilliwack, South Surrey) now open their titles with the searched phrase ("Mobile Homes for Sale in Langley BC | 55+ & Family Parks") and their descriptions with the facts each page already states and sources: named parks, the price range (Abbotsford) and pad rent ranges (Abbotsford, Surrey, Maple Ridge, Mission, Chilliwack, South Surrey's Sea Ridge Bays strata fee). Langley's description names its parks instead of a rent range, since its own pages quote two different ranges ($600–$900 and $650–$950); that is left for a sourced fix. The "New, used, manufactured or modular homes" section moves from the end of each page to just after the introduction ("new mobile homes for sale", "modular homes for sale" and "manufactured homes for sale" had 436 impressions and 1 click over the same four weeks); its sources stay at the end under "Sources: new, used and modular homes".

**D-365 (2026-11-19) Review of an outside audit: what held up, what didn't, and the fixes.** Jared: "Merge and can you review these below and how we can improve?" (a list of seven suggestions). Checked against Search Console (Oct 21–Nov 18) and Google results the same morning:
- "Surrey realtor", "best realtor in surrey bc" and similar show position 1–2.5 with almost no clicks. On Google those searches show ads, then the map pack with Jared's Business Profile, then directories (REW, realtor.ca, RankMyAgent). Search Console counts the website link in the map pack at the pack's position, so these are Business Profile results, not homepage rankings. People who pick him there call or open the profile. A homepage title change does not reach them. The Business Profile (reviews, photos, posts) is what moves them.
- Abbotsford duplexes and 55+ condos at "position 5" are automated searches (`gibbons intitle:acquire site:.ca` and the like), not people. Not changed.
- Reviews and the bio pages show up under searches for his own name, where six of his pages fill the results; that is good, so the three bio pages (about, jared, the South Surrey agent page; 5–17% shared text) stay. Zoning: /zonings.html and the A1 post answer different searches (A2/I2/RU-1 zone codes vs "a1 zoning"), so both stay.
- Review stars: Google does not show stars for a business's reviews of itself (its "self-serving reviews" rule for LocalBusiness and Organization markup), so review markup would not add stars. Not added.
- The 403s were crawled on the old site before Nov 12; those addresses now forward. The 404s and redirects are old MLS listing pages that no longer exist, which is normal and costs little. /selling.html was last crawled Oct 3; it goes first in tomorrow's indexing requests.
- Desktop's average position (14.7 vs mobile 9.4) is an average over more pages showing; it is not a ranking loss.
- The 4 videos already have upload dates (Google's check started Nov 14). The videos are on YouTube, so YouTube's own page wins video results; separate video pages are not worth building now.
- /home-evaluation.html and /selling.html are linked from every page already (1,986 pages).
Fixes: (1) A paused Google Ads campaign ("south surrey seller add") lands on /realtor/south-surrey, which was a 404 on the new site; it now forwards to the South Surrey agent page. (2) Snippets with a reason to click: the homepage, Reviews and South Surrey agent descriptions say "rated 5.0 on Google" (no count, so it does not go stale; the site says 94 reviews and Google now shows 95); the Reviews title becomes "Jared Gibbons Reviews | 5.0 on Google | South Surrey REALTOR®"; Clayton guide ("clayton surrey" at 5.7, "east clayton" at 11.5, 1 click from 510) and the zoning guide (964 impressions, 6 clicks) get sharper titles or descriptions. (3) The A1 post ("a1 zoning" at 5.7, 0 clicks from 150) gets the title "What Is A1 Zoning in BC? Uses, Lot Sizes & ALR Rules" and a table comparing the seven A1 and A-1 zones in the zoning guide (lot sizes, homes and main uses), with links to each zone card. The table is a hand-built post-context entry (`manual: true`); tools/build_post_context.py now keeps such entries.

**D-366 (2026-11-19) Page-by-page review of the pages ranking in the top 20.** Jared: "how many pages in the top 20 spot? can you go through each one and figure out how to improve them". Search Console, first full week on the new site (Nov 12–18): 488 pages had impressions and 311 averaged a top-20 position, but most of those averages are lifted by automated searches (`... intitle:(acquire ...) site:.ca`) and by old-site listing addresses that now forward. With those searches removed, 34 pages have 20 or more real impressions in the top 20 (11 in the top 10), plus 26 with 10–19. Each was checked against its real searches and its page. Changes:
- Titles and descriptions matched to what people search: Guildford houses ("guildford surrey real estate", "house for sale guildford"), North Vancouver schools (the searches are for private schools; the description now leads with the 13 private and independent schools), Ladner open houses ("This Weekend", like the other open-house pages), East Newton houses ("houses for sale east newton", "newton surrey homes for sale"), the GIS page ("white rock gis map", "surrey gis"; the title now names the cities it covers) and the Langley holiday post ("fort langley tree lighting 2026" at 6.9: the title, H1 and description lead with it and its date, Saturday November 28).
- The school-list heading "Independent schools in X" is now "Private and independent schools in X" on all 22 school lists (people search "private schools"), in tools/build_school_lists.py too.
- Research-note wording that had reached public pages is rewritten for buyers: the Langley mobile homes fee line ("one unit's copy for one park, not Langley figures and not park rates"), the Langley parks page ("NEITHER range is adopted here, and no dollar figure from either is a fact", "the directory and the listings disagree — on the record"), the Langley mobile-homes-under-$500K pad-cost answer, the Langley and South Surrey presale pages ("No developer source ... supports the range", "What the listings we checked show"), and Westham Island. The Langley parks page's condo/townhome/land template sections (it has no condos) are now one section: the six parks, how owning a park home works, pad rent increases, schools and transit, sources.
- Template headings that named a topic as if it were a place ("Surrey Open Houses: getting around", "BC Land Buying Rules: getting around", "Broiler Farms BC: streets and boundaries") now read naturally on 19 pages (119 headings): place pages use the place ("Surrey: getting around"); topic pages use "practical steps", "where it applies", "what you're buying"; all use "details most buyers miss" and "what's changing".
Not changed: the homepage (map-pack results), Harbour Chines (its searches are for Mandarin- and Cantonese-speaking agents), the Safeway post (people want the store), and pages whose top searches are school-district names. The pages ranking 10–25 for "for sale" searches are held back by outside links and listings Google cannot read (D-365), not by their copy. Still to do: "listing-stated" and "(secondary)" source notes on about 50 pages read like research notes; a wording pass is next.

**D-367 (2026-11-19) Second pass on the top-20 pages: event results, the ALR guide, and research wording site-wide.** Jared: "what would you do to improve these seo". Three changes beyond titles:
- Event markup. The Langley holiday post ranks 6.9 for "fort langley tree lighting 2026". Four of its dated events (Aldergrove tree lighting Nov 22, Fort Langley tree lighting Nov 28, Langley Christmas Village Nov 26–Dec 24, Aldergrove Light Up Parade Dec 12) now carry Event markup from src/data/post-events.json, so they can show as event results. Times and places are the post's own; the Fort Langley hall's street number is left out because sources disagree (Glover Road vs Glover Crescent). Events without a firm place or time (Glow, Merry & Bright, the South Langley Church market) are not marked up.
- The ALR guide (88 impressions a week at 12.7 for "alr", "alr bc", "what does alr mean in real estate", "buying land in bc"; 0 clicks) was an emoji list with no facts. It is rewritten (post-rewrites.json): what ALR means, how to check a parcel (ALC map and the zoning lookup), the housing limits (500 m² principal residence, 1,000 m² fill, suite inside, 90/186 m² additional residence), subdividing and stratifying, ALR vs zoning, farm Class 9 tax, where the ALR land is locally (links to the farm pages) and a pre-offer checklist, each sourced to the ALC, BC Assessment or the Province, matching the facts already on the BC acreage rules and farms pages. Title "What Is the ALR? Buying Land in BC's Agricultural Land Reserve".
- tools/fix_research_wording_2026_11_19.py rewrites 76 research-note phrases on 62 pages ("listing-stated", "on the record", "listing text only", "not a fact") into plain buyer wording ("per the listing", "from listings"), keeping the meaning. School-level labels "(secondary)" stay.

**D-368 (2026-11-19) Mobile home financing post, open-house weekend line, Langley parks table.** Jared: "can you work on these items: 3 Rewrite the mobile home mortgage post ... 4 Open house pages showing this weekend's date ... 5 A fuller Langley mobile homes page".
- The mobile home mortgage post is rewritten (post-rewrites.json): "Mobile Home Financing in BC: Mortgages vs Chattel Loans". It covers mortgage vs chattel loan, CMHC's 5% down for new prefab homes, what lenders check on a park home (pad lease, CSA label, registry), co-op parks and bare-land strata, and a pre-offer checklist, sourced to CMHC and the BC Manufactured Home Registry and parks pages. No lender rates are quoted.
- Every open-house page now shows "This weekend: Saturday, [date] and Sunday, [date]" under the H1. The build writes it, and the Worker rewrites it on each request (src/lib/weekend.js, Vancouver time; on a Sunday it names the current weekend), so the date is never stale between builds. The ETag is dropped on those pages so a cached copy is not reused.
- The Langley mobile homes page gains "Langley mobile home parks at a glance": a table of eight parks (address, area, how you own, age rule, pets) from the parks' own listings and the park directory, with "Ask the park" where no source says, plus "Five checks before you buy in a Langley park" and an FAQ on 55+ parks. No pad rents in the table (the sources disagree, see D-364).

**D-373 (2026-11-19) Page-wide signals: a real "Updated" date, one question section, current breadcrumb names, links to weakly linked core pages.** Jared: "are the pages missing anything? ... do whatever you can by yourself first please". A check of the 41 pages reviewed in D-366 to D-372 found:

Every page except posts and utility pages now shows "Updated <date> · By Jared Gibbons, REALTOR®" under the H1, and carries WebPage markup with the same dateModified. The date is the page's sitemap <lastmod>, the day its own content last changed (D-140), so it only moves when the page does. src/integrations/lastmod.mjs leaves the date out of the hash and writes the final date into the page after the build. Guides used the market-data month (D-201) and now use the same exact date. This build used LASTMOD_REHASH once, so the date line itself did not re-date ~1,900 pages; pages whose content changed in this pass were set to Nov 19.

The D-202 merge now recognizes those headings, so the extra questions join the page's own section.
- The breadcrumb trail (and its BreadcrumbList markup) stored each page's H1 from when the trail was built. The last crumb now takes the page's current H1.
- Weakly linked core pages (links from other pages' main content): notary fees 2, Panorama Ridge guide 8, North Surrey townhomes 10, South Surrey luxury 13, North Surrey houses 23, North Surrey homes 24. 90 related pages now carry one line under their introduction, "Also worth comparing: ...", naming the ones they did not link to yet (tools/link_weak_pages_2026_11_19.py). The notary page is linked from core buying-rules, presale, mobile-home, land and sell-my-house pages whose text mentions a lawyer or notary.
Not done here. Listings Google can read need a data feed (the DDF decision is on hold). Area photos from Jared's listing folder are next: only 9 of the 41 pages have a photo of the area, and each photo needs checking against the photo rules. Area videos and area-named reviews need Jared.

**D-377 (2026-11-19) Core pages batch 6: titles that match the searches, and a map, zoning and postal-code section for Langley City.** Jared: "ok thanks can you work on the next batch of pages". The last 16 core pages ranking below 20 (Search Console, URL-prefix property, 28 days, bot searches removed). Pages with real searches changed; the rest (Grandview, the golf course pages, Bear Creek, the Cloverdale guides hub, the two South Surrey 2-bedroom pages) had too little data to act on and stay as they are.
- Titles and H1s: Fleetwood Tynehead condos and townhomes lead with "Fleetwood Condos & Townhouses" ("fleetwood condos for sale", 25.5). Campbell Valley names South Langley ("south langley real estate", "homes for sale in south langley"). The Surrey selling page says "Surrey BC" and the free evaluation ("sell my home surrey bc", 47). Guildford townhomes say "Townhouses" (D-372). Murrayville condos add "Langley BC". Surrey farms leads with "Farms for Sale in Surrey BC", since "farms for sale surrey" was landing on the Cloverdale farms page at 79 to 95. Surrey homes under $1M adds "Affordable".
- Langley City guide: people reach it searching "langley city map", "city of langley zoning map" and "langley zip code", and the page had none of the three. It now opens with a section that has the Langley City outline map, says the City and the Township are separate municipalities with separate zoning maps, points to the City's Interactive Map, PDF maps and Zoning Bylaw No. 3300, and gives the postal code prefix (V3A for Downtown Langley, with a note that the tax notice, not the postal code, settles City or Township). Sources: the City's maps-and-open-data and zoning bylaw pages, and geocoder.ca for the postal code. Title "Langley City Guide | Map, Zoning, Parks & SkyTrain".
- Links: the Langley City guide had 11 inbound links and Surrey farms 23. Two Langley pages now link to the guide and four Surrey acreage pages (Surrey, Cloverdale, South Surrey acreages, and Hazelmere) to Surrey farms, on the "Also worth comparing:" line (D-374).
tools/core_pages_6_2026_11_19.py.

**D-378 (2026-11-19) Site-wide check after the core-page batches: writer notes removed from live copy, links to the weakest pages.** Jared: "go through all the pages and tell me what we did didn't change or what we should change or that I missed". A scan of all 1,949 indexable built pages found no duplicate titles, H1s or descriptions, no broken internal links, no wrong canonicals, no images without alt text and no thin pages. It did find:
- Writer instructions left in public copy on 24 pages, e.g. "never cite 18020 as current", "(cite the number)", "cite it as adopted", "Don't use North or Northeast figures for Northwest". Each is now a plain statement of the same fact ("it replaced Bylaw 18020"); no fact changed. The same wording is still in some docs/research notes, so a generator that rebuilds notes from them could bring it back. tools/fix_writer_notes_2026_11_19.py.
- 30 pages with only one or two inbound links. Links added: White Rock townhomes (from four White Rock pages), the Yorkson guide (from four Willoughby pages), and the BC money and tax guides (FHSA, RRSP Home Buyers' Plan, minimum down payment, newly built exemption, rescission period, foreign buyers, flipping tax, speculation tax, assessment appeals) from the first-time buyer, calculator, transfer tax and Surrey, Langley and South Surrey/White Rock selling pages, as an "Also worth reading:" line. buying.html and selling.html are designed pages that don't render these lines, so they were left alone. tools/link_weak_pages_2_2026_11_19.py.
